單純形是否也最大化正規化投影體積？

條件與限制

摘要給出二十維反例，不聲稱確定真正最大者；本導讀未獨立核驗證明。

可能延伸（推論）

研究延伸推測：可研究乘積凸體如何改變投影極值，並搜尋新的最佳上界。

Original English abstract

The product of two ten-dimensional simplices has larger normalized projection-body volume than a twenty-dimensional simplex. This gives a counterexample to Brannen's proposed simplex maximum.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

097-01 / FAMILY 097

Euclidean Steinitz–Bergström 定理

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，Rᵈ 單位球中的任意既定順序有限向量序列，都能選 ± 符號使每個前綴和範數≤C√d。並推出任意零和向量族可重新排序達同階控制，確定 Euclidean Steinitz 常數為 Θ(√d)。

問題

能否使逐步累積的向量誤差只隨維度平方根增長？

Original English abstract

Every prescribed-order finite sequence of vectors in the Euclidean unit ball of ℝd has one signing for which every signed prefix has norm at most $C\sqrt d$, with C absolute and independent of the sequence length. Consequently, every indexed zero-sum family of unit-ball vectors admits an ordering with the same bound for its unsigned partial sums. This determines the Euclidean Steinitz constant up to absolute factors, $S_2(d)=\Theta(\sqrt d)$, and resolves the Euclidean Steinitz–Bergström conjecture.

編輯距離嵌入 l1 的指數量級匹配界

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，長度至多 d 字串的單位成本編輯距離，最小 l1 失真為 exp(Θ(√(log d·log log d)))。上下界常數對所有至少二符號有限字母表一致；等長二元字串已迫使下界。

問題

編輯距離可被 l1 距離近似到多高精度？

條件與限制

插入、刪除、替換均成本一；只在指數中的常數因子內匹配；本導讀未獨立核驗證明。

可能延伸（推論）

研究延伸推測：可評估用向量距離索引文字或序列資料的理論極限。

Original English abstract

We determine the exponential scale of the least ℓ1 distortion of unit-cost edit distance on all strings of length at most d. For every sufficiently large d, uniformly over finite alphabets of size at least two, the distortion lies between $\exp(c\sqrt{\log d\,\log\log d})$ and $\exp(C\sqrt{\log d\,\log\log d})$ for absolute constants $c,C\gt 0$. The lower bound already holds on binary strings of one common length. Thus the order of logarithmic distortion is sharp up to absolute constants.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

099-02 / FAMILY 099

編輯距離的有限圓障礙、二元編碼與直方圖嵌入

逐篇導讀

作者給出兩種有限圓構造，聲稱等長二元字串迫使 exp(Ω(√(log d·log log d))) 的 l1 失真。另將重疊子字串法發展成同指數量級的有限直方圖嵌入，涵蓋所有有限字母表及空字串。

問題

如何用具體構造同時證明編輯距離嵌入的障礙與上界？

條件與限制

下界構造、編碼及上界各有不同角色，摘要未聲稱精確最佳常數；本導讀未獨立核驗證明。

可能延伸（推論）

研究延伸推測：可研究可實作的直方圖特徵及二元編碼的距離損失。

Original English abstract

We give two finite-circle constructions of binary strings whose least ℓ1 distortion is $\exp(\Omega(\sqrt{\log d\,\log\log d}))$, where d bounds their length. Both constructions supply words of one common length for every sufficiently large cap. Two direct binary coding arguments transfer the constructions with absolute distortion and logarithmic block width. We also develop the overlapping-substring method of Ostrovsky and Rabani into a complete finite histogram embedding at the same exponential scale, uniformly over all finite alphabets and all words of length at most d, including the empty word.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

099-03 / FAMILY 099

二元編輯距離 l1 失真的樹構造

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，兩個獨立樹構造都產生長度≤d 的等長二元字串，迫使 exp(Ω(√(log d·log log d))) 失真。另給固定輸入長度的常數失真二元轉換，與伴隨上界共同確定對數失真量級。

問題

樹狀組合結構能否產生尖銳的編輯距離嵌入下界？

條件與限制

完整匹配結論需搭配另篇上界；轉換先固定輸入長度；本導讀未獨立核驗證明。

可能延伸（推論）

研究延伸推測：可研究序列度量中的樹障礙與跨字母表比較。

Original English abstract

We give two independent constructions of binary words of one length at most d whose ordinary edit-distance metrics require ℓ1 distortion $\exp(\Omega(\sqrt{\log d\,\log\log d}))$ for every sufficiently large d. We also prove a constant-distortion binary conversion for one prescribed input length. Together with the companion upper embedding theorem, these lower bounds determine the order of logarithmic distortion uniformly over finite alphabets with at least two symbols.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

100-01 / FAMILY 100

低於半投影面積的有限角向圓柱覆蓋

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，正四面體可由有限個具有緊緻三角形垂直底面的柱體覆蓋，總底面積低於其最小正交投影面積的一半。仿射不變性再將方向正規化版本的反例推廣至任意非退化四面體。

問題

柱體覆蓋的總底面積是否必至少等於最小投影的一半？

條件與限制

一般四面體延伸的是方向正規化界，不能混同未正規化界；本導讀未獨立核驗證明。

可能延伸（推論）

研究延伸推測：可研究凸體覆蓋界與方向分配最佳化。

Original English abstract

A regular tetrahedron admits a finite cylinder covering with compact triangular perpendicular bases whose total area is less than half its minimum orthogonal projection area. This disproves the half-area cylinder-covering conjecture. By affine invariance, the same construction gives a counterexample to the directionwise normalized half-bound for every nondegenerate tetrahedron.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

100-02 / FAMILY 100

直紋集合的有限柱體逼近

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，特定 C¹ 速度場生成的緊緻線段族可由有限柱體逼近，底面積成本不超過積分投影成本加任意正誤差。微分具相反實特徵值，其絕對值小於線段半長倒數；亦容許雙零特徵值與任意剪切。

問題

連續掃掠線段的面積成本能否由有限柱體忠實實現？

條件與限制

速度場正則性及特徵值條件不可省略，標籤集可任意緊緻；本導讀未獨立核驗證明。

可能延伸（推論）

研究延伸推測：可作幾何覆蓋反例的離散化工具，研究更一般掃掠族。

Original English abstract

We approximate compact ruled families of segments by finitely many cylinders with square intercept tiles and perpendicular-base area at most their integral projection cost plus any positive error. The velocity field is C1, and its differential has opposite real eigenvalues whose magnitudes are strictly below the inverse segment half-length; both eigenvalues may vanish. The result includes square-zero differentials with unrestricted shear and fields that pass between the two regimes. It holds for arbitrary compact label sets.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

100-03 / FAMILY 100

雙柱體覆蓋的斜率場擾動

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，擾動正四面體的雙柱體覆蓋，可形成總垂直底面積嚴格小於最小投影一半的有限覆蓋。此構造否定半面積界與方向正規化半界，後者經仿射變換適用所有非退化四面體。

Original English abstract

The two-cylinder covering of a regular tetrahedron can be perturbed to give finite covers with total perpendicular base area strictly below half its minimum projection area. 100-04 / FAMILY 100

徑向掃掠的有限三角形逼近

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，徑向對齊線段掃掠可依任意帶標記分割，為每段配置一個三角形底面的柱體覆蓋。當網格趨零，總底面積收斂至加權參數面積，並用以構造突破正四面體半投影界的覆蓋。

An explicit application covers every regular tetrahedron with total base area below half its minimum projection area, giving negative answers to the half-area question and the directionwise normalized 1-Codimensional Cylinder Covering Conjecture.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

101-01 / FAMILY 101

Original English abstract

We prove the strong isotropic constant conjecture: in each dimension, simplices are the unique maximizers of the isotropic constant among convex bodies. We also prove the sharp entropy bound $h(f)\ge m+\tfrac12\log\det\mathop{\mathrm{Cov}}\nolimits (f)$ for every log-concave probability density on ℝm, with equality precisely for invertible affine images of products of one-sided exponential laws.

102-01 / FAMILY 102

Unique Games 定理

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，任意固定 ε,δ∈(0,1/2)，均可在確定性多項式時間把 3SAT 化約為固定有限字母表的 Unique Games。可滿足輸入的完整性至少 1−ε，而不可滿足輸入的健全性至多 δ。

問題

近乎可滿足的唯一約束遊戲，是否仍難與低值實例區分？

條件與限制

字母表依固定誤差參數選取；不代表已給實際求解器；本導讀未獨立核驗證明。

可能延伸（推論）

研究延伸推測：若證明成立，可重新檢視依 UGC 建立的近似困難性門檻。

Original English abstract

We prove the Unique Games Conjecture. For every fixed $\varepsilon,\delta\in(0,1/2)$, we give a deterministic polynomial-time reduction from 3SAT to Unique Games over a fixed finite alphabet, with completeness at least $1-\varepsilon$ and soundness at most δ.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

102-02 / FAMILY 102

Max-Cut 最佳困難性的直接證明

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，在簡單無權圖上，任何固定優於 Goemans–Williamson 常數的 Max-Cut 近似因子皆為 NP-hard。此為直接困難性證明，旨在把已知演算法比例與不可超越門檻連結。

問題

最大割能否以多項式時間保證優於經典半正定近似比例？

條件與限制

NP-hard 不等於無條件排除多項式演算法；常規不可能性仍需 P≠NP；本導讀未獨立核驗證明。

可能延伸（推論）

研究延伸推測：可用於比較最大割啟發式的最壞情形保證與實際表現。

Original English abstract

We prove that approximating Max-Cut on simple unweighted graphs within any fixed factor greater than the Goemans–Williamson constant is NP-hard.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

104-04 / FAMILY 104

隨機準多項式時間的平均報酬遊戲

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，隨機演算法對每條隨機帶都以準多項式位元時間計算零門檻勝出區，正確率至少 7/8。多項式時間檢查器可證實勝區與雙方位置策略或回報失敗，重複後得到永遠正確且期望準多項式的演算法。

可能延伸（推論）

Original English abstract

We give a randomized algorithm that computes the complete zero-threshold winning set of a finite mean-payoff game with arbitrary signed integer edge weights encoded in binary. For total explicit input length L, it uses $2^{O((\log(L+2))^2)}$ bit operations on every random tape and is correct with probability at least 7/8. A polynomial-time check certifies the winning regions and positional strategies for both players or reports failure. Independent repetition therefore gives an always-correct algorithm with the same expected quasipolynomial bit bound.

105-01 / FAMILY 105

2-to-1 遊戲的完美完整性

逐篇導讀

作者聲稱，每個固定有理 δ∈(0,1)，區分完全可滿足的 2-to-1 遊戲與最優值≤δ 的遊戲是 NP-hard。字母表固定，約束以明確無權多重列表給出，完整性恰為一。

問題

2 對 1 約束是否在保留完全可滿足性時仍有任意小健全性？

條件與限制

字母表依 δ；完全完整性不等於所有輸入可滿足；本導讀未獨立核驗證明。

可能延伸（推論）

研究延伸推測：可研究近似困難性化約中對可滿足承諾的保存。

Original English abstract

We prove the 2-to-1 Games Conjecture with perfect completeness. For every fixed rational $\delta\in(0,1)$, it is NP-hard to distinguish satisfiable 2-to-1 games from games of value at most δ, with a fixed alphabet and an explicitly listed unweighted multiset of constraints.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

Original English abstract

Original English abstract

We also prove the sharp bound $|\mathop{\mathrm{supp}}\nolimits x|-\dim F_x\le3m-2$, where Fx is the minimal face of the perfect-matching polytope containing x.

Original English abstract

作者聲稱，任意有限顯式字串族都可在完整編碼輸入長度的多項式時間內，構造長度≤最優值兩倍的共同超字串。成本包含符號標籤編碼；保證屬於本文算法，並非經典最大重疊 Greedy 法。

條件與限制

可能延伸（推論）

Original English abstract

We give a deterministic algorithm that, for every finite family of explicitly represented ordinary strings, outputs a common superstring of length at most twice the optimum in time polynomial in the total encoded input size, including symbol labels. The guarantee applies to the algorithm constructed here, not the classical maximum-overlap Greedy procedure.

Original English abstract

Original English abstract

We give a uniform randomized classical algorithm for Subset Sum with bounded error and worst-case running time $O(2^{0.49n})$ on polynomial-bit inputs in a word-RAM model, where n is the number of input integers. The time bound holds on every random execution.

No differentiability across the leaves is assumed. This resolves the near-boundary Birkhoff conjecture in the stated smooth class.

作者聲稱，每個由有限實相似壓縮生成的自相似測度，其 Hausdorff 維度等於 min{1,hRW/χ}。精確重疊可存在，壓縮比可不同且可為負，hRW 為隨機複合映射熵率，χ 為平均對數收縮率。

Original English abstract

We prove the permanence conjecture for finite weakly reversible mass-action systems with fixed positive reaction rates. 質數階 Ryser 猜想的平衡反例

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：對充分大質數 q 構造交會的 (q+1) 部均勻超圖，其覆蓋數為 q+1，且各部大小相同。

問題

交會超圖的覆蓋數是否一定比部數少一？

條件與限制

質數需充分大；平衡性指每部非孤立頂點數相同。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可研究超圖覆蓋、相交族與整數規劃的極端案例。

Original English abstract

For every sufficiently large prime q, we construct a finite intersecting $(q+1)$-partite $(q+1)$-uniform hypergraph with covering number $q+1$ and exactly $q+1$ nonisolated vertices in each part. This disproves Ryser's covering conjecture, even for intersecting hypergraphs with equally sized parts.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

162-02 / FAMILY 162

Ryser 覆蓋猜想的反例

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：在特定質數及充分大奇數次方參數下，構造覆蓋數等於部數的交會均勻超圖。

問題

Ryser 界在交會超圖這個基本情形是否成立？

條件與限制

質數需滿足 s≡2 mod 3，次方門檻可依 s 改變。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可比較數論參數如何驅動超圖覆蓋反例。

Original English abstract

For every sufficiently large prime $s\equiv2\pmod3$ and every sufficiently large odd integer n, with the threshold depending on s, we construct an intersecting $(s^n+1)$-partite $(s^n+1)$-uniform hypergraph with covering number $s^n+1$. This disproves Ryser's covering conjecture in its intersecting case.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

問題

可能延伸（推論）

Original English abstract

We prove Hindman's finite sums and products conjecture: for every finite coloring of the positive integers and every positive integer k, there is a k-element set whose nonempty subset sums and nonempty subset products all have the same color.

165-01 / FAMILY 165

完全圖的交叉數

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：證明 Harary–Hill 公式，精確計算任意完全圖的普通平面交叉數。

問題

把完全圖畫在平面上最少需要多少邊交叉？

條件與限制

普通交叉數允許曲線邊，不能混同直線交叉數。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可作為圖繪製與互連佈局的理論下界案例。

Original English abstract

We prove the Harary–Hill conjecture: for every positive integer n, the ordinary crossing number of the complete graph Kn is $\displaystyle \frac14\left\lfloor\frac n2\right\rfloor \left\lfloor\frac{n-1}{2}\right\rfloor \left\lfloor\frac{n-2}{2}\right\rfloor \left\lfloor\frac{n-3}{2}\right\rfloor.$

Original English abstract

條件與限制

可能延伸（推論）

Original English abstract

We prove the circulant Hadamard conjecture: a real circulant Hadamard matrix has order 1 or 4. As a consequence, Barker sequences of length greater than one exist exactly at lengths 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 11, 13, proving the Barker-sequence conjecture.

182-01 / FAMILY 182

相交型多項式差的統一次數冪次界

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：若整數多項式模每個正整數都有根，則避開其非零值作為差的集合具有固定冪次密度損失。

問題

局部同餘可解性何時迫使密集集合出現多項式差？

條件與限制

首項係數為正且次數至少二；衰減指數只依次數，常數可依多項式。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可分析加法結構與多項式重現的定量界。

Original English abstract

An integer polynomial is intersective if it has a root modulo every positive integer. For each degree k ≥ 2, we prove that there is an exponent $c_k\gt 0$ such that every set $A\subseteq\{1,\ldots,N\}$ whose differences avoid all nonzero values $h(1),h(2),\ldots$, where h is an intersective polynomial of degree k with positive leading coefficient, satisfies $|A|=O_h(N^{1-c_k})$. The implied constant may depend on h, but the power-saving exponent depends only on its degree.

182-02 / FAMILY 182

質數參數多項式差的冪次改善

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：對模每個正整數都有單位根的固定多項式，主張避開 h(p) 質數差值的集合大小有固定冪次節省。

問題

把多項式輸入限制為質數後，是否仍強迫差值重現？

條件與限制

證明依賴同系列 Dirichlet L 函數零點自由半平面結果。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可探索質數分布與加法組合的交互應用及依賴鏈核驗。

Original English abstract

Let h be a fixed integer polynomial of degree at least two with positive leading coefficient, having a unit root modulo every positive integer. We prove that any set $A\subseteq\{1,\ldots,N\}$ whose differences avoid all nonzero values $h(p)$ at primes satisfies $|A|\le C_hN^{1-c_h}$, where $c_h\gt 0$ and $C_h\ge1$ depend only on h. Thus the local unit-root condition gives a fixed power saving even when polynomial arguments are restricted to primes. The proof uses the companion zero-free half-plane theorem for Dirichlet L-functions to obtain the required prime-distribution estimates.

特徵二的 Kaplansky 直接有限性反例

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：構造特徵二有限域與有限呈示群，滿足 ab=1、ba≠1；並導出非 sofic 群及單射非滿射細胞自動機。

問題

群環的單側逆與細胞自動機滿射性猜想是否普遍成立？

條件與限制

多項結論共用同一構造，需核對其依賴鏈與域假設。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可研究離散動力、可逆運算與群近似性的關聯。

Original English abstract

We disprove Kaplansky's direct-finiteness conjecture by constructing a finite field K of characteristic two, a finitely presented group G, and finite sums $a,b\in K[G]$ with $ab=1$ but $ba\ne1$. The group G is nonsofic. The same elements define a cellular automaton on KG that is injective but not surjective, disproving Gottschalk's surjunctivity conjecture.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

197-03 / FAMILY 197

群環行列式猜想的反例

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：構造整數群環矩陣，在有理群環上可逆，但 Fuglede–Kadison 行列式嚴格介於零與一。

問題

群環矩陣的廣義行列式是否總受預期的整數式下界限制？

條件與限制

群為有限生成，且對數積分有限；這不是普通有限矩陣行列式。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可探索 L² 不變量、算子代數與群環譜量。

Original English abstract

We disprove the unrestricted group-ring Determinant Conjecture. We construct a finitely generated group G and a square matrix over $\mathbb Z[G]$ that is invertible over $\mathbb Q[G]$ and has Fuglede–Kadison determinant strictly between zero and one. The logarithmic integral defining the determinant is finite.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

197-04 / FAMILY 197

奇特徵的 Kaplansky 直接有限性反例

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：對一個指定奇質數 p，以 p⁴ 元域及含撓有限生成群構造 ab=1、ba≠1。

問題

直接有限性失敗是否不限於特徵二？

條件與限制

只聲明一個指定奇質數且群含撓，非所有奇特徵或無撓群。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可比較不同特徵下單側可逆性與細胞自動機反例。

Original English abstract

We construct a counterexample to Kaplansky's direct-finiteness conjecture in odd characteristic. For one specified odd prime p, we obtain a field K of order p4, a finitely generated group G containing torsion, and finite sums $a,b\in K[G]$ with $ab=1$ but $ba\ne1$. The same elements define a cellular automaton on KG that is injective but not surjective.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：構造有限維複代數，其有限維模具有無界的有限投射維數，主張否定小 finitistic 維數猜想。

問題

所有有限投射維數能否被一個代數相關常數統一控制？

「小」指有限生成／有限維模範疇，不是代數本身無窮維。

可能延伸（推論）

Original English abstract

199-01 / FAMILY 199

Auslander–Reiten 猜想的明確反例

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：在特定特徵二函數域上構造非投射 Gorenstein 投射模，與自身及代數的所有正次 Ext 均消失。

問題

高階擴張全消失是否足以強迫模為投射？

條件與限制

係數域具特定參數，並聲稱任意域擴張後仍成立。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可研究 Ext 檢測、同調剛性與 Gorenstein 投射模。

Original English abstract

We disprove the Auslander–Reiten conjecture for Artin algebras. We construct a finite-dimensional algebra Λ over $k=\mathbb F_2(q,H_1,H_2)$ and a finite-dimensional nonprojective left module Z such that $\mathop{\mathrm{Ext}}\nolimits ^i_\Lambda(Z,Z)=\mathop{\mathrm{Ext}}\nolimits ^i_\Lambda(Z,\Lambda)=0$ for every i > 0. The module is Gorenstein-projective, so the same example also disproves the Gorenstein-projective conjecture. Both counterexamples persist after every extension of k.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

199-02 / FAMILY 199

Tachikawa 第二猜想的反例

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：構造對稱有限維代數上的非投射模，所有正次自擴張消失，並推及數個 Nakayama 與傾斜猜想。

問題

對稱代數中的無自擴張條件能否判定投射性？

條件與限制

多個附帶反例來自伴隨自同態代數，需分別核對假設。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可整理同調代數猜想的推論關係與可用判準界線。

Original English abstract

We disprove Tachikawa's second conjecture by constructing a finite-dimensional symmetric algebra over $k=\mathbb F_2(q,H_1,H_2)$ with a finite-dimensional nonprojective module whose self-extension groups vanish in every positive degree. The associated endomorphism algebra also gives counterexamples to the classical, generalized, and strong Nakayama conjectures, the Auslander–Gorenstein conjecture, and the Wakamatsu tilting conjecture. These conclusions persist after every extension of k.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

200-01 / FAMILY 200

Artin 型 Lex-Plus-Powers Betti 定理

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：在特徵零中，主張含齊次正則序列的理想可由同 Hilbert 函數且各階 Betti 數更大的 lex-plus-powers 理想控制。

問題

如何同時控制商環的分次大小與自由解析複雜度？

條件與限制

正則序列各次數至少二；不直接涵蓋正特徵。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可把同調不變量上界化成單項式理想的可計算組合問題。

Original English abstract

We prove the Eisenbud–Green–Harris and lex-plus-powers conjectures over every characteristic-zero field for homogeneous regular sequences of any positive length, with degrees at least two. For every homogeneous ideal containing such a sequence, the corresponding lex-plus-powers ideal has the same Hilbert function and at least as large a graded Betti number in every homological and internal degree.

Original English abstract

We prove the Eisenbud–Green–Harris conjecture over every characteristic-zero field for homogeneous regular sequences of any positive length, with degrees at least two. Thus every homogeneous ideal containing such a sequence has the Hilbert function of a monomial ideal containing the corresponding pure powers.

Original English abstract

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：將有界缺陷的 Morita 有限性提升到 Witt 向量環，並涵蓋指定完備離散賦值環的分歧情形。

Original English abstract

We prove integral Donovan finiteness: for every prime p and positive integer M, the blocks of all finite groups with defect groups of order at most M have only finitely many Morita equivalence classes over $W(\overline{\mathbb F}_p)$. The same bounded-defect finiteness holds over each fixed complete discrete valuation ring of characteristic zero with algebraically closed residue field of characteristic p, including ramified rings.

典範 Foulkes–Howe 映射的二次穩定界

逐篇導讀

稿件主張： a≥2、b≥a(a−1) 時典範映射為滿射，穩定門檻不依 V 的維度。

問題

對稱冪交換映射多久後必然穩定為滿射？

條件與限制

結論適用明確二次門檻以上，不涵蓋其下全部參數。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可為符號表示計算設定可證的穩定區間。

Original English abstract

For every finite-dimensional complex vector space V, we prove that the canonical Foulkes–Howe map $\mathop{\mathrm{Sym}}\nolimits ^b(\mathop{\mathrm{Sym}}\nolimits ^a V)\longrightarrow\mathop{\mathrm{Sym}}\nolimits ^a(\mathop{\mathrm{Sym}}\nolimits ^b V)$ is surjective whenever a ≥ 2 and $b\ge a(a-1)$. This gives a quadratic stabilization bound independent of $\dim V$.

211-01 / FAMILY 211

臨界 FK–Ising 地圖的隨機漫步與 Liouville 布朗運動

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：平穩漫步連同隨機曲面收斂到單位面積 √3 量子球上的 Liouville 布朗運動。

問題

離散隨機曲面的粒子漫步如何產生量子幾何上的連續擴散？

條件與限制

需伴隨幾何與電性結果；時鐘加速恰為邊數取決於指定半邊與平穩測度慣例。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可連結隨機幾何、二維量子重力與擴散模擬。

Original English abstract

We prove that stationary random walks on critical spherical FK–Ising planar maps converge to Liouville Brownian motion on the ordinary unit-area $\sqrt3$-quantum sphere, using the geometric and electrical results of the spectral companion. The walk chooses uniformly among all incident half-edges, retaining loops and multiple edges. With the corner measure as the stationary law, the deterministic time acceleration is exactly the number of map edges. For any deterministic metric scale giving the metric-measure limit, convergence retains that same surface and any fixed finite number of conditionally independent walks with their time parameters.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

問題

離散隨機曲面的振動與熱擴散是否一致趨向連續極限？

條件與限制

依賴共形及度量伴隨論文與指定 LQG 輸入；熱跡遠離 t=0 比較。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可研究熱核、譜維度及離散—連續算子的數值對照。

Original English abstract

Using the conformal and metric-measure companion results and the stated Brownian/Liouville quantum gravity inputs, we prove spectral convergence for critical spherical FK–Ising maps to Liouville Brownian motion on the ordinary unit-area $\sqrt3$-quantum sphere. The discrete walk has total attempt rate one, uses every map edge including loops and multiplicities, and has the corner measure as its stationary law. Accelerating time by the number of map edges gives joint convergence of the metric-measure space, all ordered eigenvalues with multiplicities and padding, and the heat trace locally uniformly at strictly positive times. The conductivity and clock constant are one in these conventions.

條件與限制

使用伴隨 Bessel 高度與 pin 極限；收斂在局部負 Sobolev 空間。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可研究共形場理論、自旋相關多點函數與連續場模擬。

Original English abstract

Using the companion critical Bessel-height and pin-limit theorems, we prove that at the mass-defined critical inverse temperature of the nearest-neighbor XY model on the square lattice, the spin field on a square with boundary angles fixed to zero converges along the full sequence to the full-variance imaginary exponential of a zero-Dirichlet Gaussian free field with stiffness $2/\pi$. The normalization uses the exact center magnetization and the lattice Green-function factor. Convergence holds in law in $H^{-3}_{\mathrm{loc}}((-1,1)^2)$; all mixed moments and joint laws of fields smeared against smooth compactly supported test functions in $(-1,1)^2$ converge as well.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

216-04 / FAMILY 216

平面 XY 模型相關長度的本質奇異性

逐篇導讀

稿件主張：從有質量側接近臨界點時，√(b_c−b) log(1/m(b)) 趨向有限正常數。

問題

條件與限制

依指定熱力學及遠距離極限定義質量，常數依模型而異。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸：可區分 BKT 與一般二階相變，設計溫度掃描及尺度擬合。

Original English abstract

We prove the Berezinskii–Kosterlitz–Thouless essential singularity for the correlation length of the nearest-neighbor cosine XY model on the square lattice. Let $m(b)$ be the mass obtained by taking first the free-box thermodynamic limit and then the separation limit along a coordinate axis. As the inverse temperature b approaches bc from the massive side, $\displaystyle \sqrt{b_c-b}\log\frac{1}{m(b)} \longrightarrow A_{\mathrm{XY}},$ where $A_{\mathrm{XY}}$ is a finite, strictly positive model-specific constant.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

227-01 / FAMILY 227

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，零外場 SK 模型在 β=1 的平衡與淬火自相關，對 Gaussian 和 Rademacher 耦合具有同一聯合隨機極限。每點更新率為一，時間尺度 n^(2/3)、自相關放大 n^(1/3)；從獨立公平自旋出發的淬火極限，隨等待時間增長趨近平衡極限。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：建立臨界自旋玻璃老化實驗的有限尺寸資料縮放測試。

Original English abstract

We prove joint functional convergence of the stationary and quench autocorrelations of zero-field Sherrington–Kirkpatrick heat-bath dynamics at inverse temperature β = 1, with the same random limit for Gaussian and Rademacher couplings. Each site has a rate-one clock, mean spin autocorrelations are multiplied by n1/3, and waiting times and lags are measured in units n2/3. The quench starts from independent fair spins, and convergence is uniform on compact sets of positive waiting times and lags. The quench limit is selected by these initial states and relaxes to the stationary limiting autocorrelation as the waiting time tends to infinity.

臨界 SK 自相關的函數普適性

Original English abstract

We prove that the stationary spin autocorrelation of the zero-field Sherrington–Kirkpatrick model at inverse temperature β = 1 has a common functional scaling limit for Gaussian and Rademacher couplings. With rate-one heat-bath clocks at every site, time is scaled by n2/3 and the mean spin autocorrelation is multiplied by n1/3. 227-05 / FAMILY 227

Original English abstract

The same statement holds for every deterministic integer sequence $k_n=o(n^{5/3})$ of uniform-site update attempts.

Original English abstract

Reconstruction occurs exactly when $d\lambda^2\gt 1$, with d = b in the regular model; there is non-reconstruction at equality for either sign of the channel parameter. The Poisson advantage is averaged over trees and spins without conditioning on survival. This resolves the all-degree three-state regular-tree prediction. Combining the Poisson theorem with known tree-to-graph and algorithmic results gives the exact weak-recovery threshold for the symmetric three-community sparse stochastic block model with independent uniform labels, fixed within- and between-community rates $a,b\gt 0$, and mean degree $(a+2b)/3\gt 1$: recovery is possible exactly when $(a-b)^2\gt 3(a+2b)$. 230-02 / FAMILY 230

SLE 的顯式精確 Hausdorff 規範函數

逐篇導讀

稿件提出 d=1+κ/8、h(r)=rᵈ[log log(1/r)]^((2−d)/2)，聲稱它對 0<κ<8 的弦型 SLE 給出有限正片段測度。它並聲稱先前猜測的迭代對數一次方規範，在這些片段上不是 σ-有限。

問題

條件與限制

公式只在充分小半徑使用；σ-有限性的否定是對另一個特定規範，不能混為無測度。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：比較兩種迭代對數修正對數值分形測度估計的影響。

Original English abstract

For each fixed $0\lt \kappa\lt 8$, let $d=1+\kappa/8$. The gauge $h(r)=r^d(\log\log(1/r))^{(2-d)/2}$ at sufficiently small radii almost surely gives positive finite Hausdorff measure to every nontrivial positive-time compact segment of chordal SLEκ. This gives an explicit solution to Schramm's Hausdorff-measure problem in this parameter range. The entire trace has finite expected measure in every bounded disk. The exponent-one iterated-logarithm gauge suggested by Schramm is not sigma-finite on any such segment.

For random 3-SAT on n Boolean variables, with independent uniformly signed clauses on three distinct variables sampled with replacement, we prove that the first unsatisfiable prefix has variance $\Theta(n)$. The upper bound removes the logarithmic loss in the earlier variance estimate; the matching lower bound follows from Wilson's transition-width theorem.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

235-03 / FAMILY 235

隨機 k-SAT 首次不可滿足時間的變異數

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，固定 k≥4 時，首次不可滿足索引 Hₙ 的變異數為 Θₖ(n)。本篇對 k=3 給出線性下界與 O(n log n) 上界，並說明伴隨論文另將後者改善為線性。

問題

子句大小如何影響首次失去可滿足性的樣本波動？

條件與限制

不能把伴隨論文的 3-SAT 改善誤寫成此篇自身上界；截斷版本另有適用敘述。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：比較不同 k 的臨界波動，追蹤對數損失來自何種估計步驟。

Original English abstract

Let Hn be the index of the first unsatisfiable prefix in random k-SAT on n variables, with independent uniformly signed clauses using k distinct variables and sampled with replacement. For every fixed k ≥ 4, we prove $\mathop{\mathrm{Var}}\nolimits (H_n)=\Theta_k(n)$. For k = 3, the variance is bounded below by a positive multiple of n and above by a constant multiple of $n\log n$; the companion paper on random 3-SAT sharpens this to $\Theta(n)$. The same upper bounds proved here hold after clipping at any fixed positive multiple of n.

235-04 / FAMILY 235

計算隨機 3-SAT 的門檻

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，均勻隨機 3-SAT 的極限門檻是可計算實數，可由單一有限確定機器逼近到任意精度。方法公平搜尋刪除估計給出的下方證書與階層軟壓力給出的上方證書，不需要可計算的有限尺寸收斂速率。

問題

門檻即使存在，是否仍能以停機演算法任意精準求得？

條件與限制

可計算不代表多項式時間或實務高效率；門檻存在的優先權仍歸原文所引 Carenini。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：研究數值上下界如何轉化為可驗證的門檻證書。

Original English abstract

The limiting satisfiability threshold of uniform random 3-SAT is a computable real. We credit Gaia Carenini [[4]](https://eccc.weizmann.ac.il/report/2026/229/) with priority for resolving the satisfiability conjecture, which establishes the threshold's existence. We prove that one finite deterministic machine can approximate it to any prescribed accuracy. A deletion estimate gives explicit lower certificates, while a finite hierarchical approximation of the soft pressure gives upper certificates at every larger rational density. A fair search through these certificates halts without requiring a computable rate of finite-size convergence.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

Original English abstract

For every integer d ≥ 3 and inverse temperature β ≥ 0, the free zero-field ferromagnetic Ising measure on the infinite d-regular tree is a factor of IID exactly when $\tanh\beta\le(d-1)^{-1/2}$. We prove the positive implication, including equality, resolving the conjecture of Nam, Sly and Zhang; the strict converse is known. The spin factor can be chosen to commute with every tree automorphism on every label input.

稿件聲稱，2ᵈ 張牌的 Thorp 洗牌只需 Θ(d) 次完整洗牌便混合。1600d 次後完整排列的總變差距離一致趨零，而支持集計數給出 2d−O(1) 下界。

問題

Thorp 洗牌讓整副牌接近均勻排列究竟需要多少次？

條件與限制

是 d→∞ 的最佳階結果，1600 與 2 並非相同的尖銳常數。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：以完整排列而非單張牌位置來評估洗牌模擬品質。

Original English abstract

We prove that the Thorp shuffle on $2^d$ cards mixes in $\Theta(d)$ complete shuffles. The full permutation law after $1600d$ shuffles converges to uniform in total variation as $d\to\infty$, uniformly over the initial deck, while a support count gives a lower bound of $2d-O(1)$.

512d 改善依賴伴隨轉移定理；物理洗牌與整輪座標掃描不可混算。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

Original English abstract

For the Thorp shuffle on $2^d$ cards, we prove that the worst-start total-variation distance after $32800d$ physical shuffles tends to zero as $d\to\infty$. Combining our fixed-list estimate with the companion Fourier transfer improves this bound to $512d$ shuffles. These results follow from bounds on partially observed permutation laws in random coordinate frames.

連續統假設對指定範疇性門檻的障礙

逐篇導讀

稿件在 CH 下構造 Löwenheim–Skolem 數為 ℵ₀ 的抽象初等類，雖於充分大基數皆具範疇性，在 beth_{ω₂} 卻至少有兩個不構模型。它據此聲稱，若 ZFC 一致，指定為 beth_{(2^ℵ₀)⁺} 的門檻版本不可由 ZFC 證明。

Original English abstract

含計數的無選擇多項式時間不足以刻畫 P

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，在無序有限結構上，含計數的 choiceless polynomial time 無法表達所有多項式時間查詢。反例是固定二元詞彙中的 F₃ 線性相容性查詢，可多項式時間決定，卻不能在該完整計數形式系統中定義。

問題

不任意選擇元素且容許計數的邏輯，是否足以描述全部 P 問題？

條件與限制

是描述複雜度的表達力分離，不是 P 與 NP 的分離。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：以線性代數任務測試無序資料邏輯語言的表達界限。

Original English abstract

We prove that choiceless polynomial time with counting does not capture polynomial time on unordered finite structures, confirming the noncapture conjecture of Blass, Gurevich and Shelah. A linear-consistency query over 𝔽3 in a fixed binary vocabulary is decidable in polynomial time but not in the full counting formalism.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

243-02 / FAMILY 243

有見證的對稱選擇嚴格強於含計數的無選擇時間

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，加入 witnessed symmetric choice 會嚴格提升含計數 choiceless polynomial time 的表達力。只含一次此種選擇的固定句子，就能在所有有限輸入上定義原形式系統無法定義的布林查詢。

問題

受控且可見證的選擇是否真正增加無序計算的能力？

條件與限制

此結果比較兩種特定形式系統，不表示新系統已完整刻畫 P。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：研究資料對稱性、選擇機制與可表達演算法之間的交換。

Original English abstract

We prove that witnessed symmetric choice strictly increases the expressive power of choiceless polynomial time with counting. A fixed sentence with one witnessed-choice occurrence defines a Boolean query on every finite input that is not definable in the original counting formalism.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

244-01 / FAMILY 244

分割原理不推出選擇公理

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，若 ZF 一致，則 ZF 加分割原理、序數索引族的選擇、以及否定完整選擇公理也一致。另在每個可數傳遞 ZFC 模型上構造同序數的傳遞對稱模型，且不增加地模型元素的可數序列。

問題

分割原理是否已暗含完整選擇公理？

條件與限制

屬相對一致性與對稱模型構造；「沒有新可數序列」只針對地模型元素。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：精確區分集合論中不同弱選擇原理的強度。

Original English abstract

We prove that the Partition Principle does not imply the Axiom of Choice: if ZF is consistent, then so is ZF with the Partition Principle, Choice for ordinal-indexed families, and the negation of the Axiom of Choice. Separately, over every countable transitive model of ZFC, we construct a transitive symmetric model of this theory with the same ordinals and no new countable sequences of ground elements.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

245-01 / FAMILY 245

純型別系統的弱與強正規化

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，只要純型別系統對所有合法脈絡中的合法表達式皆弱 β-正規化，便也皆強 β-正規化。化簡包含型別註記內部，且不需要 functionality 假設。

問題

每個表達式存在可終止化簡路徑，是否足以保證所有化簡路徑終止？

條件與限制

量詞涵蓋整個系統的全部合法表達式；不是單一表達式弱正規化就推出強正規化。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：分析依賴型別語言的化簡終止性與型別檢查基礎。

Original English abstract

We prove that every weakly β-normalizing pure type system is strongly β-normalizing. Both properties quantify over all legal expressions in all valid contexts, and reduction acts inside type annotations. No functionality hypothesis is required. This resolves the β-Barendregt–Geuvers–Klop conjecture.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

允許有限核，不能將結論誤寫為必定忠實作用或任意邊界拓樸皆成立。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

247-01 / FAMILY 247

無窮有限呈示剩餘有限的 2-群

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，伴隨論文的週期 Steinberg 群 Γ=St₁₂(R) 是剩餘有限。其至 St₁₂(F₂) 同態之核是一個無窮、通常意義有限呈示、剩餘有限的 2-群，而元素階數無共同上界。

問題

所有元素皆為有限二冪階的無窮群，能否同時有限呈示且由有限商分辨？

可能延伸（推論）

推論：研究有限商逼近與局部扭元素條件如何共存。

Original English abstract

We prove that the infinite, ordinarily finitely presented periodic Steinberg group $\Gamma=\mathop{\mathrm{St}}\nolimits _{12}(R)$ of a companion paper is residually finite. Its finite-index subgroup $G=\ker(\Gamma\to\mathop{\mathrm{St}}\nolimits _{12}(\mathbb F_2))$ is infinite, ordinarily finitely presented, and residually finite, and every element of G has finite 2-power order. The orders of its elements are unbounded. Phases of long words in the companion's graded algebra allow finite degree truncations to detect all elements of Γ, including the central kernel.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

247-02 / FAMILY 247

無窮有限呈示週期群

逐篇導讀

稿件構造一個通常意義下有限呈示、無窮、但每個元素皆有限階的群。它另聲稱構造 F₂ 上有限呈示且無窮維的非含幺 nil 代數，該代數 Jacobson 根但非冪零，其含幺化也有限呈示且代數性。

問題

有限呈示加上逐元素有限性是否足以強迫整體有限？

條件與限制

nil 指每元素冪零，nilpotent 指整體有共同冪零階；兩者不能混同。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：以反例辨別群與代數中有限描述、局部有限性及整體有限維的關係。

Original English abstract

We construct an infinite group with an ordinary finite presentation in which every element has finite order, answering the finitely presented Burnside question negatively. We also construct an infinite-dimensional finitely presented nonunital nil associative algebra over 𝔽2 that is Jacobson radical but not nilpotent. Its unitization is finitely presented, algebraic, and infinite-dimensional. These algebras answer the finitely presented nil- and radical-algebra nilpotence questions and the finite-presentation version of Kurosh's algebraic finiteness question negatively.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

Original English abstract

稿件聲稱，有限生成群的字問題可判定，恰當且僅當它嵌入一個有限呈示單群。這把演算法可判定性與極強的代數包絡性質對應起來。

條件與限制

結論限有限生成群；嵌入存在不自動提供高效率字問題演算法。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

Original English abstract

We prove the Boone–Higman conjecture. A finitely generated group has decidable word problem if and only if it embeds in a finitely presented simple group.

250-02 / FAMILY 250

251-01 / FAMILY 251

251-02 / FAMILY 251

強 Ulam 穩定性刻畫可和性

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，可數離散群可和，恰等價於任意複 Hilbert 空間上充分準確的酉近似表示，均一致接近同一空間的真正表示。新主張是 Kazhdan 可和穩定定理的逆向，且無窮維空間是必要部分。

條件與限制

強穩定性在所有 Hilbert 空間量化；不能只檢查有限維表示便套用。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

Original English abstract

A countable discrete group is amenable if and only if it is strongly Ulam stable: every sufficiently accurate unitary almost representation, on any complex Hilbert space, is uniformly close in operator norm to a genuine representation on the same space. We prove the converse to Kazhdan's amenable stability theorem, answering the question of Burger, Ozawa, and Thom. The inclusion of infinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces is essential.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

255-01 / FAMILY 255

實質多循環群的擬等距辨識

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，有限生成群若與有限生成的實質多循環群擬等距，則自身也實質多循環。其結論容許對應的可解 Lie 群宿主與假設中的宿主不同。

問題

只看大尺度距離，能否辨識多循環群的代數類型？

條件與限制

不是聲稱群同構或必定落在同一 Lie 群格點中；僅保證類型。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：由大型網路的粗幾何辨識可解群式結構。

Original English abstract

We prove that every finitely generated group quasi-isometric to a finitely generated virtually polycyclic group is virtually polycyclic. This resolves the lattice-recognition conjecture of Eskin, Fisher and Whyte, which allows the ambient solvable Lie group in the conclusion to differ from the one in the hypothesis.

260-01 / FAMILY 260

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，三維單端初始資料在指定能量、衰減與陷獲條件下，滿足 m≥Q 與 r_A≤m+√(m²−Q²)。僅在 r_A>Q 時才宣稱多項式質量形式；非極端等號在額外連通最外層視界假設下，還原原資料為雙荷 Reissner–Nordström 的空間樣切片。

問題

條件與限制

允許非零 ADM 動量與一般第二基本形式；不得省略能量、無源電磁及等號的視界假設。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：核對數值相對論初始資料的面積界與球對稱黑洞等號極限。

Original English abstract

Under the stated energy, decay, and trapping hypotheses, we prove the sharp charged spacetime Penrose upper-area inequality for one-ended three-dimensional initial data with source-free electric and magnetic fields. The theorem allows arbitrary second fundamental form, nonzero ADM momentum, and disconnected boundary. Writing m for invariant ADM mass, Q for total charge magnitude, and rA for the minimum-enclosing-area radius, the bound is m ≥ Q and $r_A\le m+\sqrt{m^2-Q^2}$. The polynomial mass bound $m\ge(r_A+Q^2/r_A)/2$ is asserted only when $r_A\gt Q$. When m > Q, equality under the stated connected, outermost, outer-area-minimizing future-horizon hypotheses identifies the original data as a smooth spacelike slice of dyonic Reissner–Nordström, including smooth attachment at the future horizon.

Original English abstract

Using the companion neutral spacetime Penrose theorem exactly at its stated strong-decay, future-trapped, positive-area, and future-timelike scope, we prove the sharp purely electric upper-area inequality in every spatial dimension n ≥ 4 for one-ended charged data satisfying the charged dominant energy condition and $\mathop{\mathrm{div}}\nolimits _g E=0$, with the specified decay, integrability, and finite-flux assumptions. The data may have arbitrary interior topology and ADM momentum, with a future or past trapping sign chosen independently on each boundary component. Positive enclosing area and strict ADM timelikeness are conclusions. The polynomial mass rearrangement is asserted only when the $(n-2)$nd power of the enclosing-area radius exceeds $|Q|$. When $m\gt |Q|$, equality under the stated connected, outermost, outer-area-minimizing future-horizon hypotheses recovers the original metric, second fundamental form, and electric field as a global spacelike slice of a Reissner–Nordström–Tangherlini exterior. Original English abstract

We prove sharp spacetime Penrose inequalities for invariant ADM mass and minimum enclosing area in three and four spatial dimensions. Under their respective designated-end conventions, the neutral numerical results allow arbitrary second fundamental form, nonzero momentum, disconnected weakly future trapped boundary, and finitely many ends. Finally, for each fixed transverse-traceless seed and prescribed decaying solution branch, we prove a local Schwarzschild–anti-de Sitter inequality.

逐篇導讀

稿件聲稱，指定軸對稱單端電真空類滿足 m²≥A/(16π)+Q²/2+π(Q⁴+4J²)/A，其中 J 包含電磁貢獻。毋須極大切片假設；嚴格面積分支上的等號，在指定視界條件下辨識為次極端雙荷 Kerr–Newman 的合適空間樣切片。

旋轉與電磁場同時存在時，質量面積界是否仍能尖銳成立？

條件與限制

可能延伸（推論）

推論：檢查旋轉帶電初始資料的物理面積分支與等號幾何。

Original English abstract

We prove the sharp Kerr–Newman Penrose inequality $\displaystyle m^2\ge \frac{A}{16\pi}+\frac{Q^2}{2} +\frac{\pi(Q^4+4J^2)}{A}.$ Here $Q^2=Q_e^2+Q_b^2$, and J is the conserved total angular momentum, including its electromagnetic contribution. The result applies to smooth axisymmetric electrovacuum exteriors in the stated one-ended topological and decay class, with a connected outermost, outer-area-minimizing future marginally outer trapped boundary, Coulomb electromagnetic asymptotics, zero ADM momentum, and the explicitly assumed physical-area condition $A\ge4\pi\sqrt{Q^4+4J^2}$. No maximality assumption is made. On the strict area branch, equality within this class characterizes the original data as an admissible spacelike exterior slice of a subextremal dyonic Kerr–Newman spacetime, with the boundary mapped smoothly to a future-horizon cross-section or the bifurcation sphere.

逐篇導讀

稿件構造軸對稱電真空外部，當 J 採裸引力 ADM 角動量、電磁場只需 O(r⁻²) 且首項隨角度變化時，可違反 Kerr–Newman 面積質量界。反例總電與磁荷皆零，仍具有指定良好視界，另有不能實現為相應 Kerr–Newman 切片的等號例。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：避免在數值資料中混用不同角動量定義而錯判黑洞界。

Original English abstract

We construct smooth axisymmetric electrovacuum exteriors that violate the Kerr–Newman Penrose inequality when J is the bare gravitational ADM angular momentum and the electromagnetic fields have only $O(r^{-2})$ decay, allowing angularly varying leading tails. The examples have zero total electric and magnetic charge even though both electromagnetic fields are nonzero. They have a connected outermost, outer-area-minimizing future marginally outer trapped boundary and lie strictly on the physical area branch. We also obtain equality examples whose original data admit no Kerr–Newman spacelike realization with the corresponding mass, angular momentum, and charges. These counterexamples do not refute formulations using conserved total angular momentum with its electromagnetic correction or stronger Coulomb asymptotics.

是 Riemannian 數值界，不能單憑本篇宣稱一般含第二基本形式的時空剛性。 此為稿件主張，本次未獨立核驗完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

推論：為高維可能奇異的視界面積計算提供幾何基礎。

Original English abstract

The metric extends smoothly through the possibly singular frontier; neither spin nor frontier connectedness is assumed. The proof follows the conformal-flow approach of Bray, Bray–Lee, and Bi–Zhu.

有能隙正方網格基態的多項式 PEPS 近似

逐篇導讀

論文主張：對 L×L 正方晶格上的一致有能隙最近鄰 Hamiltonian，唯一基態存在 bond dimension 為 L 多項式的 PEPS 近似。正規化後全域向量誤差至多 L⁻¹；僅假設全體 Hamiltonian 有 gap，常數對固定局部維度、強度及 gap 的模型一致。

問題

二維基態能否以多項式 bond dimension 在全域向量範數中近似？

條件與限制

這是存在性定理，未給高效張量構造、最佳化或收縮演算法；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：研究 PEPS 模型容量與壓縮；需額外證明才能主張實務量子模擬加速。

Original English abstract

We prove that the unique ground state of a uniformly gapped nearest-neighbor Hamiltonian on an $L\times L$ square lattice admits a projected entangled-pair state approximation with bond dimension polynomial in L and global vector error at most L−1 after normalization. Only the gap of the full Hamiltonian is assumed. The result is an existence theorem, with constants uniform over Hamiltonians of fixed local dimension, interaction strength, and gap.

266-01 / FAMILY 266

六維互不偏基底的最大數量

逐篇導讀

論文主張：ℂ⁶ 中兩兩互不偏正交基底的最大數目為 3，對應六維 Zauner 猜想。排除第 4 組的部分使用計算輔助證明，依指定 binary64 算術與編譯器條件，完整執行驗證流程以排除任意複基底。

問題

六維量子量測是否容許超過三組兩兩均勻重疊的正交基底？

條件與限制

上界依完整覆蓋、捨入誤差合約與執行紀錄；本次沒有重跑該大型計算；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：設計量子量測教具、比較 tomography 基底及研究認證數值搜尋。

Original English abstract

We prove that the maximum number of mutually unbiased orthonormal bases in ℂ6 is three, resolving Zauner's dimension-six MUB conjecture. The upper bound is computer-assisted: under the stated binary64 arithmetic and compiler conditions, a complete execution of the documented verification pipeline excludes four arbitrary complex bases.

266-02 / FAMILY 266

六階複 Hadamard 矩陣的精確 Fourier 證書

逐篇導讀

論文主張：Tao 三次類別以外的每個六階複 Hadamard 矩陣，在 (1,1,1,−1,−1,−1) 的所有排列上均有指定角色和為零。另提供只用整數與有理數的證書排除 ℂ⁶ 的 7 組互不偏基底，再藉 Weiner 完成定理得到上界 5；包含完整驗證器。

問題

能否用精確代數證書控制六階 Hadamard 的 Fourier 消去與 MUB 上界？

條件與限制

本篇自身的 MUB 上界為 5，不應誤寫成它單獨證 N(6)=3；排除特定 Tao 等價類；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：構建精確量子設計檢查器並比較區間式與有理數式證書。

Original English abstract

We prove the Fourier-vanishing conjecture of Matolcsi, Ruzsa, and Weiner: every complex Hadamard matrix of order six outside Tao's cubic equivalence class has a vanishing character sum at every permutation of $(1,1,1,-1,-1,-1)$. We also give an exact certificate excluding seven mutually unbiased bases in ℂ6; by Weiner's completion theorem, this yields an upper bound of five. Both certificates use only integer and rational arithmetic, and the complete verifier is included.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

267-01 / FAMILY 267

稀薄硬球 Bose 氣體在正溫度的 Bose–Einstein 凝聚

逐篇導讀

論文主張：三維稀薄硬球氣體在正溫度具有 Bose–Einstein 凝聚。固定排斥距離與足夠小但固定密度後，存在不依體積的正溫度，使精確正則 Gibbs 態於熱力學極限中有正凝聚比例，凝聚於常數軌域。

問題

真實互動硬球模型在固定正溫度能否嚴格產生宏觀軌域佔據？

條件與限制

溫度為存在性且可依密度；非完整臨界溫度公式，也非任意密度或任意勢能；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：為稀薄 Bose 氣體有限溫度模型提供基準，另分析臨界尺度與實驗參數。

Original English abstract

We prove Bose–Einstein condensation at positive temperature in the three-dimensional dilute hard-sphere gas. For each fixed exclusion distance and every sufficiently small fixed density, there is a strictly positive temperature, independent of the volume, at which the exact canonical Gibbs state has a positive condensate fraction in the thermodynamic limit. The condensate occupies the constant orbital.

267-02 / FAMILY 267

稀薄硬球 Bose 氣體的量子耗盡與動量分布

逐篇導讀

論文主張：三維硬球 Bose 氣體零溫耗盡粒子的完整縮放動量分布符合 Bogoliubov 預測。固定密度先取熱力學極限，再取稀薄極限，對所有純或混合基態一致；非零動量縮放佔據測度總質量為 8/(3√π)，耗盡比例為該係數乘 √(ρa³) 加較小階項。

問題

除了總耗盡量，能否控制互動 Bose 基態的整個縮放動量分布？

條件與限制

三維硬球、零溫與指定極限順序；不宣稱有限體積或有限密度的誤差常數；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：比較 Bogoliubov 理論與動量分辨量測、建立量子耗盡數值基準。

Original English abstract

We prove the full scaled Bogoliubov momentum distribution for the depleted particles in the three-dimensional hard-sphere Bose gas at zero temperature. The thermodynamic limit is taken at each fixed density before the dilute limit, uniformly over all pure and mixed ground states. All thermodynamic accumulation values have the same dilute asymptotic: the limiting nonzero-momentum occupation measure has total mass $8/(3\sqrt\pi)$, giving the depletion fraction $\frac{8}{3\sqrt\pi}\sqrt{\rho a^3}+o(\sqrt{\rho a^3})$ for density ρ and hard-sphere exclusion distance a.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

267-03 / FAMILY 267

固定有界排斥勢的量子耗盡

逐篇導讀

論文主張：三維 Bose 氣體在固定有界、非負、徑向、有限範圍且正散射長度 a 的勢能下，基態耗盡比例為 8√(ρa³)/(3√π)+o(√(ρa³))。固定 ρ 先取熱力學極限，再令 ρ↓0；對基態密度矩陣一致，不要求佔據量的熱力學極限唯一。

問題

Bogoliubov 耗盡係數能否從硬球拓展到固定有界排斥勢？

條件與限制

徑向、非負、有限範圍及正散射長度屬必要範圍；採累積值而非假定極限唯一；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：比較不同微觀勢的散射長度普適性，需另建立有限密度誤差估計。

Original English abstract

We prove the Bogoliubov quantum-depletion asymptotic for the ground state of a three-dimensional Bose gas with a fixed bounded, nonnegative, radial interaction of finite range and positive scattering length a. The thermodynamic limit is taken at each fixed density ρ before the dilute limit. Every thermodynamic accumulation value of the fraction outside the constant mode is $\frac{8}{3\sqrt\pi}\sqrt{\rho a^3}+o(\sqrt{\rho a^3})$ as $\rho\downarrow0$. The assertion is uniform over ground-state density matrices and does not require the occupation to have a unique thermodynamic limit.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

267-04 / FAMILY 267

稀薄 Bose 氣體中對密度一致的凝聚下界

逐篇導讀

論文主張：對三維固定有界可測、非負、徑向、有限範圍且非幾乎處處為零的勢能，常數軌域凝聚比例具有正下界。此界對所有充分小密度與 0≤T≤ρ² 一致，熱力學極限中勢能及密度保持固定。

問題

能否找到不隨稀薄參數消失、且涵蓋小正溫度區間的凝聚比例下界？

條件與限制

溫度區間是原文正規化的 T≤ρ²；不等於最佳臨界溫度，也不涵蓋任意勢；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：建立低溫凝聚的穩健區間，並研究物理單位與實驗尺度的轉換。

Original English abstract

For each fixed bounded measurable, nonnegative, radial interaction of finite range in three dimensions that is not zero almost everywhere, we prove a positive lower bound on the constant-orbital condensate fraction that is uniform over all sufficiently small densities and all temperatures between zero and the square of the density. The interaction and density remain fixed in the thermodynamic limit.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

267-05 / FAMILY 267

稀薄硬球氣體的基態凝聚

逐篇導讀

論文主張：三維稀薄硬球 Bose 氣體的每一個基態都具有 Bose–Einstein 凝聚。每個充分小但固定氣體參數下，常數軌域在熱力學極限佔正粒子比例；下界可不依氣體參數，並涵蓋任意複基態。

問題

凝聚是否對全部硬球基態成立，而不只是特定實正基態或精選序列？

Original English abstract

We prove Bose–Einstein condensation in every ground state of a dilute three-dimensional hard-sphere Bose gas. At every sufficiently small fixed gas parameter, the constant orbital contains a positive fraction of the particles in the thermodynamic limit. The fraction can be chosen independently of the gas parameter, and the conclusion holds for arbitrary complex ground states.

spin-1 Heisenberg 鏈的邊界場能隙

問題

Original English abstract

We prove a uniform spectral gap for odd open spin-one antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chains with the same fixed magnetic field at both endpoints. The field $h=3/5$ gives a gap greater than $\log(10)/392$ on every chain of $2L+1$ sites with L ≥ 960. Tasaki's index theorem then gives index −1 for every subsequential local limit of these boundary-selected ground states.

269-01 / FAMILY 269

球面 Laughlin 能隙的一致穩定性

逐篇導讀

論文主張：在擴張圓球上、填充率 1/3 的費米 Laughlin V₁ Hamiltonian，加入足夠弱且投影至最低 Landau 能階的有界實純量單體勢後，唯一基態與一致 gap 仍保留。每對投影係數為 1、磁通 q=3(N−1)，擾動門檻與 gap 對足夠大粒子數及 sup 範數≤1 的勢輪廓一致。

問題

球面 Laughlin gap 是否抵抗一般有界弱單體擾動？

條件與限制

使用 companion 的未擾動 Fock-space gap；常數為存在性，作用限定最低 Landau level；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：分析分數量子 Hall 模型對雜質勢的穩健性，需另連結真實幾何與實驗尺度。

Original English abstract

We prove that the fermionic Laughlin V1 Hamiltonian at filling 1/3 on expanding round spheres retains a unique ground state and a uniform spectral gap under sufficiently weak bounded real scalar one-body potentials projected to the lowest Landau level. With coefficient one for each pair projector and Laughlin flux $q=3(N-1)$, the gap and perturbation threshold are uniform for all sufficiently large particle numbers and all potential profiles of supremum norm at most one. The result uses the uniform unperturbed Fock-space gap and gives existential constants.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

269-02 / FAMILY 269

Fock 空間不等式與 Laughlin 譜隙

逐篇導讀

論文主張：對完整 V₁ 交互作用，球面填充率 1/3 的費米 Laughlin 態在足夠大系統有 gap 至少 1/25。更一般地，大磁通 Q 下整個最低 Landau level Fock 空間滿足 H_Q²≥γH_Q，其中固定 γ>1/25，且與粒子數無關。

問題

Laughlin 母 Hamiltonian 的能隙能否在熱力學尺度保持，並擴到整個 Fock 空間？

條件與限制

每對投影的係數正規化為 1；足夠大磁通門檻，非任意 Coulomb Hamiltonian；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：建立量子 Hall 拓樸相與弱擾動穩定性的可用譜下界。

Original English abstract

We prove the spherical fermionic Laughlin spectral-gap conjecture for the full V1 interaction. With coefficient one for each pair projector, the gap above the Laughlin state at filling 1/3 on the round sphere is at least 1/25 for all sufficiently large systems. More generally, $H_Q^2\ge\gamma H_Q$ for some fixed $\gamma\gt 1/25$ on the entire lowest-Landau-level Fock space for all sufficiently large flux Q, independently of particle number.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

We prove that the relative $\mathop{\mathrm{SU}}\nolimits (2)$ BFSS Hamiltonian has infinitely many positive eigenvalues tending to infinity, with square-integrable eigenvectors. 271-01 / FAMILY 271

三維有限範圍 Heisenberg 鐵磁體的 Bloch 定律

逐篇導讀

論文主張：對固定正量子 spin、非負對稱有限範圍且支撐生成 ℤ³ 的耦合，自發磁化低溫缺額滿足 Bloch T³ᐟ² 定律。精確領先係數由單 magnon 二次色散矩陣的行列式決定，允許空間各向異性。

問題

能否由互動量子自旋嚴格導出自旋波磁化定律及係數？

條件與限制

先熱力學極限、再零場右導數、最後低溫；不能直接微分零場自由能漸近式；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：比較各向異性 magnon 有效模型與磁化溫度律；動態輸運仍需額外理論。

Original English abstract

We prove Bloch's T3/2 law for the spontaneous magnetization of three-dimensional quantum Heisenberg ferromagnets at every fixed positive quantum spin. The result holds for every nonnegative symmetric finite-range interaction whose support generates ℤ3, including spatially anisotropic couplings. The leading magnetization deficit has the exact coefficient determined by the determinant of the quadratic one-magnon dispersion. The thermodynamic limit is taken before the right field derivative at zero, and the low-temperature limit is taken last.

271-02 / FAMILY 271

Bloch 定律的第一個晶格修正

逐篇導讀

論文主張：三維最近鄰量子 Heisenberg 鐵磁體對每個固定 S=1/2,1,3/2,…，磁化缺額與完整理想 magnon 密度之差為 o(β⁻⁵ᐟ²)。除領先 Bloch 項外，得到修正 3ζ(5/2)(βS)⁻⁵ᐟ²/(128π³ᐟ²)。

問題

從連續二次色散近似回到離散晶格，第一個可控磁化修正是多少？

條件與限制

最近鄰、三維與固定 spin；體積、零場、低溫的順序不可互換；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：量化低溫晶格色散效應與磁化擬合；不把此階修正誤稱為全部 magnon 交互作用。

Original English abstract

We prove the first lattice correction to Bloch's law for the three-dimensional nearest-neighbor quantum Heisenberg ferromagnet at every fixed spin $S=\tfrac12,1,\tfrac32,\ldots$. The spontaneous-magnetization deficit agrees with the full ideal-magnon density up to $o(\beta^{-5/2})$. In addition to the leading Bloch term, this gives the correction $3\zeta(5/2)(\beta S)^{-5/2}/(128\pi^{3/2})$. The magnetization is the right derivative at zero field of the thermodynamic pressure; the volume limit precedes the field derivative, and the low-temperature limit is taken last.

271-03 / FAMILY 271

三維量子 Heisenberg 鐵磁體的球面磁化律

逐篇導讀

論文主張：三維最近鄰各向同性量子鐵磁體，在固定正 spin 與充分低但固定正溫度，偶數週期盒增大時，對稱零場磁化依矩收斂至方向均勻、大小確定且為正的分布。該大小等於無窮體積 pressure 的零場右導數。

問題

零場對稱態平均磁化可為零時，巨觀磁化的分布如何呈現有序？

條件與限制

使用自伴線性組合的矩描述，不假設非對易自旋分量可聯合量測；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：教學上區分對稱混合態與選向自發磁化，以及研究巨觀序參量波動。

Original English abstract

We prove the spherical magnetization law for the three-dimensional nearest-neighbor isotropic quantum Heisenberg ferromagnet at every fixed positive quantum spin and every sufficiently low fixed positive temperature. As the even periodic cubes grow, the symmetric zero-field magnetization converges in moments to a uniform direction with a deterministic positive magnitude. This magnitude equals the right derivative at zero field of the infinite-volume pressure. The law is expressed through self-adjoint linear combinations of the spin components and requires no joint measurement of noncommuting observables.

271-04 / FAMILY 271

量子 Heisenberg 鐵磁體的自發磁化

274-01 / FAMILY 274

Ancillas start in zero, only one output qubit is measured, and all final garbage is unrestricted. This resolves Moore's parity conjecture in the measured-output model.

逐篇導讀

論文主張：三維電子 Coulomb 譜下確界的近似，在正整數核電荷以二進位編碼時為 QMA-hard。原子核位於相異有理位置、電子數採 unary，對全部反對稱連續態與自旋部門取下確界；確定性多項式歸約產生至少 1 的閾值間距。

問題

不預先選軌域基底的連續電子問題，本身是否具有量子驗證複雜度障礙？

可能延伸（推論）

Original English abstract

We prove that approximating the electronic Coulomb spectral infimum in three-dimensional space is QMA-hard when positive integer nuclear charges are encoded in binary. The nuclei have distinct rational positions, the electron number is unary, and the energy is minimized over all antisymmetric continuum states and spin sectors. A deterministic classical polynomial-time reduction produces instances with threshold separation at least one. The nuclear charges may be exponentially large, but every output has polynomial bit length.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

Original English abstract

Its universal rate is proportional to $\delta^5/(1+\log(|\mathcal A||\mathcal B|))$, where $\mathcal A,\mathcal B$ are the answer alphabets. For each fixed question distribution, we also obtain an explicit cubic rate in δ.

We prove that the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) approaches the ground-state energy per spin of the Gaussian zero-field Sherrington–Kirkpatrick model when system size tends to infinity first and circuit depth then increases. For every accuracy, some finite depth and deterministic angles, independent of system size and disorder, achieve that accuracy in the limiting expected energy per spin using the standard cost Hamiltonian and transverse-field mixer. This proves the eventual Parisi-optimality conjecture of Basso, Farhi, Marwaha, Villalonga, and Zhou in its fixed-parameter thermodynamic formulation. We give no quantitative bound on the required depth or efficient angle-selection procedure.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

281-02 / FAMILY 281

零溫 Sherrington–Kirkpatrick 序參數的完整支撐

逐篇導讀

論文主張：純零場 SK 模型零溫 Parisi 泛函的每個可容許可積極小者，在 [0,1) 都有完整相對 Stieltjes 支撐。即所有小於 1 的 overlap 尺度都無支撐缺口，採 covariance ξ(t)=t²/2 正規化。

問題

零溫序參數是否真的涵蓋所有 overlap 尺度，而非只有部分區間？

條件與限制

限定純、零場、指定正規化與可積極小者；不直接提供數值密度公式；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：研究 full RSB 結構、Parisi 數值解與最佳化能量景觀。

Original English abstract

We prove that every admissible integrable minimizer of the zero-temperature Parisi functional for the pure, zero-field Sherrington–Kirkpatrick model has full relative Stieltjes support on $[0,1)$. Thus its support has no gaps at any overlap scale below one. We use the covariance normalization $\xi(t)=t^2/2$.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

282-01 / FAMILY 282

四維 operational 量子場論的尺度與共形對稱

逐篇導讀

論文主張：對指定四維酉、正能量 QFT 類別，尺度對稱可提升為局部共形對稱。假設尺度維度譜離散且下有界、有限重數、原始場有限尺度支撐、相容局部網實現與物理 dilation current 的 Ward identities；據此改善應力張量成對稱、守恆且無跡，保持平移與 Lorentz 電荷。

問題

在完整局部算子與電流假設下，scale invariance 是否強迫 conformal invariance？

條件與限制

結論是局部 Ward identities；整個有界局部網的全域共形群作用仍是另一問題；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：梳理場論固定點、應力張量改善與尺度／共形論證的必要條件。

Original English abstract

We prove that scale symmetry implies local conformal symmetry for a class of four-dimensional unitary, positive-energy quantum field theories with a Poincaré- and scale-invariant vacuum. The class has a discrete, bounded-below spectrum of scaling dimensions with finite multiplicities, and each original field has finite scaling support. The original fields and their adjoints have compatible affiliated realizations in a causally commuting bounded local net, and a physical local dilatation current generates scale transformations through its local Ward identities. The operational framework admits sharply localized reconstructed fields after their joint products and compatible affiliated realizations in the same net have been established. Under these hypotheses, the stress tensor admits a symmetric, conserved, traceless improvement with unchanged translation and Lorentz charges. The corresponding conformal currents satisfy unbroken local Ward identities on the physical field algebra. The conclusion is local; integration to a global conformal action on the whole bounded net remains a separate question.

283-01 / FAMILY 283

由 Boolean oracle 進行多項式時間酉算子合成

逐篇導讀

論文主張：對每個 n，可僅由 n 在多項式時間生成一個量子 oracle 電路，適當選 Boolean oracle 後，近似任意 n-qubit 酉通道至完整 diamond-norm 誤差≤1/2。使用固定 H,T,T†,CNOT 閘，qubits、閘數、oracle 呼叫和查詢長度皆多項式。

問題

把目標資訊藏在 Boolean oracle 中後，一個通用電路能否合成任意酉通道到常數誤差？

條件與限制

oracle 可依目標酉算子而變；不給高效古典程序建造它，不能當一般無 oracle 酉編譯器；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：研究 oracle 資訊存取、量子合成與資料載入成本的分界。

Original English abstract

We give a positive answer to the constant-error formulation of the Aaronson–Kuperberg unitary synthesis problem. For every n, a quantum oracle circuit generated in polynomial time from n alone can approximate the channel of every n-qubit unitary to full diamond-norm error at most 1/2, after a suitable Boolean oracle is chosen. Using the fixed gates $H,T,T^\dagger,\mathrm{CNOT}$, the circuit has polynomially many qubits, elementary gates, and oracle calls, and its oracle queries have polynomial length. The oracle may depend on the target unitary; the theorem does not give an efficient classical procedure for constructing it.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

284-01 / FAMILY 284

隨機與量子查詢複雜度近四次方分離

逐篇導讀

論文主張：構造總 Boolean 函數，使 bounded-error 隨機查詢複雜度 R 與量子 Q 有近四次方分離。這排除所有 α<4 的通用 R(f)=O((1+Q(f))^α) 界，顯示已知四次方上界的指數最佳，並反駁三次方猜想。

問題

對所有輸入都有定義的 Boolean 函數，量子查詢相對隨機查詢最多能改善多少？

條件與限制

計算的是最壞輸入 bit queries、逐輸入錯誤≤1/3；非完整執行時間或所有資料結構成本；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：設計查詢模型分離例與辨析 oracle 加速能否轉成端到端加速。

Original English abstract

We construct total Boolean functions with a nearly quartic separation between bounded-error randomized and quantum query complexity. Writing these complexities as $\mathrm R(f)$ and $\mathrm Q(f)$, the examples rule out every universal bound $\mathrm R(f)=O((1+\mathrm Q(f))^\alpha)$ with α < 4. Thus the known quartic upper bound has the optimal exponent, disproving the conjectured cubic bound. Both complexities count worst-case bit queries with error at most 1/3 on every input.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

Original English abstract

We prove a bounded spectral recovery theorem for a von Neumann algebra with a faithful normal state whose centralizer consists only of scalars. A positive averaged squared norm for an arbitrary bounded Hilbert-space operator on shrinking modular spectral bands can be recovered on uniformly bounded algebra elements with shrinking spectral support. We apply this theorem to spectral intertwining rigidity and obtain an alternative proof of bicentralizer triviality for type III1 factors with separable predual.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

291-01 / FAMILY 291

單位穩定有限情況下 amenable 作用的等變 Jiang–Su 穩定性

逐篇導讀

論文主張：可數離散 amenable 群作用在簡單、可分、單位、無窮維、nuclear、stably finite 且已 Z-stable 的複 C* 代數上，會在 cocycle conjugacy 意義下吸收 Jiang–Su 代數的平凡作用。對 tracial-state simplex 上的作用不加限制，處理 Szabó 猜想 A 的此一範圍。

問題

已知非等變 Z-stability，是否會自動提升為群作用的等變吸收？

條件與限制

單位、穩定有限及原本 Z-stable 是假設；不是任意群或任意 C* 代數作用；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

可能延伸（需另外論證或實作）：分類 amenable 動力系統與等變算子代數，研究跡空間作用的可容範圍。

Original English abstract

Every action of a countable discrete amenable group on a simple, separable, unital, infinite-dimensional, nuclear, stably finite complex C∗-algebra that is already Jiang–Su stable absorbs the trivial action on the Jiang–Su algebra up to cocycle conjugacy. No restriction is imposed on the action on the tracial-state simplex. This proves the unital, stably finite case of Szabó's Conjecture A on automatic equivariant Jiang–Su stability.

條件與限制

需保留 strict comparison 的擴展泛函定義與非 elementary 條件；不能只用有限跡版本替代；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

Original English abstract

We prove that strict comparison in the extended-functional sense implies Jiang–Su absorption for separable simple nuclear non-elementary C∗-algebras. This resolves the corresponding implication of the Toms–Winter regularity problem, including nonunital algebras and allowing unbounded traces. More generally, every separable nuclear C∗-algebra whose Cuntz semigroup is almost unperforated and fully almost divisible absorbs the Jiang–Su algebra.

291-04 / FAMILY 291

跡投影方法與一致 property Γ

逐篇導讀

論文主張：對簡單、可分、單位、無窮維、nuclear、stably finite 且有跡的 C* 代數，一致跡完成的一致跡 ultrapower 具有 real rank zero 可推出 uniform property Γ。另主張 strict comparison 推出單位情況的 Z 吸收，並在有限 target rank 比較及有界緻密定義跡等假設下處理 stably projectionless 類。

問題

條件與限制

各結論的跡有界性、緊 normalized base 與比較測試範圍不同，不能合併成無條件普遍結論；此為摘要層導讀，未在本次獨立驗證完整證明。

可能延伸（推論）

Original English abstract

For a simple, separable, unital, infinite-dimensional, nuclear, stably finite C∗-algebra with traces, real rank zero of the uniform tracial ultrapower of its uniform tracial completion implies uniform property Γ. This answers Problem XXI of Schafhauser, Tikuisis and White affirmatively. Independently, strict comparison implies Jiang–Su absorption for simple, separable, unital, infinite-dimensional nuclear algebras, resolving the unital Toms–Winter conjecture. Under comparison tested on traces of finite target rank, we also obtain uniform property Γ and absorption for simple, separable, nuclear, stably projectionless algebras whose densely finite traces are all bounded and have a nonempty compact normalized base.

300-01 / FAMILY 300

任意最大交換子代數上的近似 paving

逐篇導讀

論文主張：每個複 von Neumann 代數的最大交換子代數都滿足 Popa–Vaes approximation-paving 猜想。自伴算子是某些自伴近似子的強極限；近似子範數≤原範數 3 倍，各自可用至多 Cε⁻⁶ 個投影得到相對自身範數 ε 誤差的 norm paving。

Original English abstract

302-01 / FAMILY 302

逐篇導讀

對有限帶基點子複形，MU 零限制在充分大的 smash 冪中，於含固定正比例受限因子的乘積聯集上穩定消失。由此得到立方體值映射的保邊界壓縮；槽位叢嵌入平凡叢的秩嚴格小於來源秩兩倍時，可把正比例槽位置於邊界。

如何把局部 MU 消失提升為相容的大冪壓縮。

條件與限制

輸入為緊緻可度量空間；原有邊界槽位逐點固定，秩等號情形有反例。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：提供平均維度與算子代數比較理論中的拓撲壓縮工具。

Original English abstract

We prove that, in sufficiently large smash powers, an $MU$-null restriction to a finite pointed subcomplex becomes stably null on the entire union of products with a fixed positive proportion of restricted factors. This coherent vanishing theorem yields boundary-preserving compression of cube-valued maps on every compact metrizable input space. When the torus slot bundle embeds continuously into a trivial bundle of rank less than twice the source rank, the compression places a positive proportion of slots on their boundaries in arbitrarily large powers and fixes every original boundary slot exactly. An example at equality shows that the strict rank inequality cannot be removed.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

302-02 / FAMILY 302

比較半徑等於平均維度的一半

逐篇導讀

本文主張，無限緊緻可度量空間上任意極小同胚的交叉積，其比較半徑等於平均維度一半，亦包含無窮大。零平均維度、小邊界性、Jiang–Su 穩定性與有限核維度彼此等價；此時核維度至多一。

問題

Phillips–Toms 猜想的整數作用情形。

條件與限制

要求極小同胚與無限緊緻可度量空間，並非任意群作用。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：用動力系統的平均維度判斷交叉積的分類正則性。

Original English abstract

We prove the integer-action case of the Phillips–Toms conjecture: for every minimal homeomorphism of an infinite compact metrizable space, the radius of comparison of its crossed product equals one half of its mean dimension, including equality at infinity. For these systems, zero mean dimension and the small boundary property are equivalent to Jiang–Su stability and to finite nuclear dimension of the crossed product; in this case its nuclear dimension is at most one.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

303-01 / FAMILY 303

弱純無窮性與 O∞ 吸收

逐篇導讀

若複 C* 代數的每個正元素皆適當無窮，本文主張該代數強純無窮。對正合代數，若所有正元素的同一固定有限放大皆適當無窮，即可去掉放大；可分核情形因而吸收 O∞。

問題

Kirchberg–Rørdam 關於一般與強純無窮性的比較問題。

條件與限制

固定放大推論使用正合性；吸收結論使用可分性與核性，不需單位或單純性。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：用正元素比較判定非單純代數的 O∞ 穩定性。

Original English abstract

We prove that every complex C∗-algebra in which each positive element is properly infinite is strongly purely infinite. This answers the ordinary-to-strong part of Kirchberg–Rørdam's comparison question. For exact algebras, we also prove that proper infiniteness of one fixed finite amplification of every positive element implies proper infiniteness of each positive element. Consequently, every separable nuclear algebra with this fixed-amplification property absorbs $\mathcal O_\infty$, without assumptions of unitality or simplicity.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

所有有限維度的 Hilbert–Smith 猜想

逐篇導讀

本文主張，局部緊緻、第二可數的 Hausdorff 群，若忠實且聯合連續地作用於指定有限維流形，必為 Lie 群。因而排除非 Lie 局部緊緻群的此類作用。

Original English abstract

We prove the Hilbert–Smith conjecture in every finite dimension: every locally compact second-countable Hausdorff group acting faithfully and jointly continuously on a connected Hausdorff second-countable finite-dimensional topological manifold without boundary is a Lie group.

305-01 / FAMILY 305

四維圓盤嵌入的純邊界障礙

逐篇導讀

本文構造緊緻定向光滑四維流形中的有限個圓盤映射，具有帶標架代數對偶球面，但邊界圓不張成互不相交的局部平坦圓盤。即使不指定同倫類與輸出標架，仍構成無基本群限制之圓盤嵌入猜想的反例。

問題

無基本群假設的四維圓盤嵌入問題。

條件與限制

由構造推出二生成自由群及含它的群不是 Freedman–Quinn 好群。

Original English abstract

We disprove the four-dimensional disc embedding conjecture without a fundamental-group hypothesis, even when no homotopy classes or output framings are prescribed. We construct a compact oriented smooth four-manifold containing finitely many disc maps with framed algebraic dual spheres whose boundary circles bound no disjoint locally flat discs. Consequently, the free group on two generators is not good in the sense of Freedman–Quinn, and neither is any group containing it as a subgroup.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

305-02 / FAMILY 305

四維圓盤嵌入的帶標記張量障礙

逐篇導讀

本文構造滿足通常等變交數與約化自交條件、且有帶標架代數對偶球面的浸入圓盤。它們無法替換成保持邊界映射及誘導法標架的兩兩不交局部平坦圓盤，縱使不指定相對同倫類。

Original English abstract

We disprove the unrestricted four-dimensional disk-embedding conjecture. The obstruction holds even when the replacement disks' relative homotopy classes are not prescribed.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

317-01 / FAMILY 317

We prove the higher-dimensional Thomason model-structure conjecture of Ara and Maltsiniotis. For every $1\le n\le\infty$, the category of small strict globular n-categories admits a proper combinatorial model structure that is Quillen equivalent to simplicial sets. Its weak equivalences and fibrations are detected by the twice-extended Street nerve $\mathrm{Ex}^2N_n$, and the Quillen equivalence is given by $c_n\mathrm{Sd}^2\dashv\mathrm{Ex}^2N_n$. Thus strict higher categories model the homotopy theory of spaces in every positive finite dimension and in dimension ω.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

318-01 / FAMILY 318

一般質數下的濾過色層分裂

逐篇導讀

對 n≥1 及 p>n+1，本文構造 Lₙ₋₁L_K(n)Sₚ^∧ 的 2ⁿ 階有序濾過。各餘纖維符合經典色層分裂樣式，首階到目標的映射是標準局部化單位。

在一般質數下實現色層分裂預期的各層。

條件與限制

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：逐層分析相鄰色層局部化的重疊譜。

Original English abstract

For every n ≥ 1 and prime $p\gt n+1$, we construct a $2^n$-stage ordered filtration of $L_{n-1}L_{K(n)}S_p^\wedge$ with the classical chromatic-splitting cofibers. The map from the first stage to the target is the canonical localization unit.

318-02 / FAMILY 318

高度三的色層重疊：明確濾過與附著

逐篇導讀

對 p≥5，本文給出 L₂L_K(3)Sₚ^∧ 的八階濾過，層對應高度三的局部球面樣式。兩個帶符號 fracture 公式描述指定局部映射與相容同倫的全部附著；伴隨結果更指出首個高度一附著非零。

問題

高度三濾過各層如何真正連接。

條件與限制

Original English abstract

For every prime p ≥ 5, we construct an explicit eight-stage filtration of $L_2L_{K(3)}\mathbb S_p^\wedge$ by the local-sphere layers in the height-three chromatic-splitting pattern. The map from its first stage to the overlap is the canonical unit, and two signed fracture formulas identify all attachments for the chosen local maps and compatibility homotopy. A companion canonical-map theorem further implies that the first height-one attachment is nonzero.

318-03 / FAMILY 318

高度三強色層分裂的有理障礙

逐篇導讀

對 p≥5，本文主張球面譜的標準映射 L₀L_K(3)S→L₀L_K(2)L_K(3)S 在 π₋₃ 非零。因而高度三強色層分裂公式不成立，即使只要求底層 E(2) 局部譜等價而不指定分量映射亦然。

問題

高度三強色層分裂是否存在。

條件與限制

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：排除忽略附著資訊的強分裂模型。

Original English abstract

For every prime p ≥ 5, the canonical map $L_0L_{K(3)}S\to L_0L_{K(2)}L_{K(3)}S$ for the sphere spectrum S is nonzero on π−3. Consequently, the height-three strong chromatic splitting formula is false in this range, even as an equivalence of underlying $E(2)$-local spectra without specified summand maps.

318-04 / FAMILY 318

質數三色層重疊的有限組裝失敗

逐篇導讀

在質數三、高度三，本文主張重疊譜無法由有理、高度一及高度二局部球面，經有限次和、位移、餘纖維與 retract 組出。敘述位於導出三完備球面上的 E(2) 局部模範疇。

318-05 / FAMILY 318

逐篇導讀

對導出 p 完備球面，本文主張 p≥5 的高度 p，以及 p≥7 的高度 p+1，標準弱色層分裂映射都沒有同倫收縮映射。兩個範圍均由經典乘積 β₁^((p−1)²) 提供非零核類。

Original English abstract

For the derived p-completion of the sphere, the canonical weak chromatic splitting map has no homotopy retraction at height p for every prime p ≥ 5, and at height $p+1$ for every prime p ≥ 7. The classical sphere product $\beta_1^{(p-1)^2}$ gives a nonzero kernel class in both ranges.

We disprove the Hahn–Wilson conjecture at height two. 322-01 / FAMILY 322

Tingley 問題的肯定解答

逐篇導讀

本文主張，實 Banach 空間單位球面間的滿射等距映射，唯一延伸成滿射實線性等距映射。證明以共同支撐對齊極端弦，結合 Darbo 不動點定理與凸性估計排除不同半徑間的距離缺陷。

問題

球面距離是否決定整個實線性賦範結構。

條件與限制

假設球面映射滿射且等距；不是一般雙 Lipschitz 映射的線性化定理。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：從單位球面的純度量資料重建線性等距對稱。

Original English abstract

Every surjective isometry between the unit spheres of real Banach spaces extends uniquely to a surjective real-linear isometry, giving an affirmative solution to Tingley's problem. If distances between different radii are not preserved, we realize the positive maximal defect in a possibly enlarged pair of Banach spaces. We then align extremal chords using common supports and Darbo's fixed-point theorem and obtain a contradiction from support and convexity estimates.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

323-01 / FAMILY 323

逐篇導讀

「每個無窮維 Banach 空間皆有可分無窮維商空間」在 ZFC 中的獨立性，本文相對於 ZFC 加可測基數的一致性建立，實與複數域皆適用。若限範數稠密度 ℵ₁，則僅需 ZFC 一致性作相對假設。

問題

可分商空間問題能否由通常集合論公理決定。

條件與限制

是相對一致性／獨立性敘述，不是 ZFC 內無條件構造所有反例。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：辨識 Banach 空間分類中需額外集合論假設的命題。

Original English abstract

The separable quotient problem asks whether every infinite-dimensional Banach space has a separable infinite-dimensional quotient. We prove that this statement is independent of ZFC, relative to the consistency of ZFC with a measurable cardinal. This holds over both the real and complex fields. For spaces of norm density ℵ1, we obtain independence relative to the consistency of ZFC alone.

325-02 / FAMILY 325

完整 Crouzeix 定理：最優相似與共同正邊界表示

逐篇導讀

本文把尖銳常數二不等式推至複 Hilbert 空間上的任意有界算子及矩陣值多項式，不需可分性，並延伸至數值域閉包附近全純的矩陣值函數。有限矩陣在指定凸解析邊界域內，另得條件數至多二的最優相似與共同正邊界密度表示。

問題

一般算子的完整譜集合估計與有限矩陣的最佳相似問題。

條件與限制

邊界密度結論另要求有界凸域、正則實解析 Jordan 邊界及包含數值域。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：控制非正規算子函數演算，並設計可評估條件數的矩陣相似變換。

Original English abstract

We resolve the complete Crouzeix conjecture by proving the sharp constant-two numerical-range inequality for every bounded operator on a complex Hilbert space and every matrix-valued polynomial. No separability assumption is needed. The closure of the numerical range is a complete 2-spectral set, and the bound extends to finite matrix-valued functions holomorphic near that closure. For a finite matrix and a bounded convex domain containing its numerical range with regular real-analytic Jordan boundary, the optimal similarity making its conformal disk image contractive is attained with condition number at most two. For the similar matrix, one continuous positive boundary density of mass the identity represents the evaluation of every matrix-valued function holomorphic near the closed domain.

Original English abstract

331-01 / FAMILY 331

反身樹空間的漸近中點一致凸性與無界鑽石失真

逐篇導讀

本文構造可分反身實 Banach 空間，原範數漸近中點一致凸，卻無任何等價漸近一致凸範數。平均中點模數至少為 √(1+t²/12)−1，深度 k 的可數分支鑽石嵌入失真至少 √(1+k/12)。

問題

反身鑽石判準能否反推出漸近一致凸重賦範。

條件與限制

中點一致凸與 AUC 不同；定量下界針對指定構造。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：測試以鑽石圖嵌入辨認 Banach 空間漸近幾何的限度。

Original English abstract

We construct a separable reflexive real Banach space whose given norm is asymptotically midpoint uniformly convex but which admits no asymptotically uniformly convex equivalent norm. Its averaged midpoint modulus is at least $\sqrt{1+t^2/12}-1$, and the countably branching diamond of depth k has distortion at least $\sqrt{1+k/12}$ in this space. This gives a negative answer to the reflexive diamond converse for asymptotic uniform convexifiability.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

331-02 / FAMILY 331

線段空間中的中點透鏡

逐篇導讀

對有限分量高度無界的實線段森林對偶，以及無窮高度座標前對偶，本文把對稱透鏡中任意位移的尾端控制在 2√(R²−‖x‖²)。兩者原範數皆漸近中點一致凸，卻不能等價重賦範為 AUC；有限高度森林對偶另具反身性。

問題

透鏡幾何能提供何種凸性而不強迫 AUC。

條件與限制

x 為有限支撐中心，R 為透鏡半徑；兩種空間的反身性不可混用。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：用尾端估計分析無窮維空間的中點穩定性。

Original English abstract

For a real segment-forest dual with unbounded finite component heights and the real infinite-height coordinate predual, we bound the tail of an arbitrary displacement in a symmetric lens by $2\sqrt{R^2-\|x\|^2}$, where R is the lens radius and x is its finitely supported center. Both given norms are asymptotically midpoint uniformly convex, although neither space admits an equivalent asymptotically uniformly convex norm. The finite-height forest dual is reflexive.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

331-03 / FAMILY 331

由中點與樹能量估計可數分支鑽石的失真

逐篇導讀

本文以中點估計及直接樹能量推導鑽石圖嵌入下界。有限高度線段森林對偶與無窮高度座標前對偶中，深度 k、失真 D 的嵌入必滿足 D²≥1+k/4，並另得路徑成本與遞迴樹範數的冪型界。

問題

如何量化鑽石圖在特定樹空間中的不可低失真嵌入。

條件與限制

精確常數 1/4 屬指定兩類空間，其他樹範數須用各自冪型界。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：建立非線性嵌入演算法或幾何模型的失真下限。

Original English abstract

We derive quantitative distortion bounds for countably branching diamond graphs from midpoint estimates and direct tree energies. In the dual of a finite-height segment forest, every distortion-D embedding of the depth-k diamond satisfies $D^2\ge1+k/4$. The same bound holds in the infinite-height coordinate predual. We also obtain power-type distortion bounds for path-cost and recursive tree norms.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

331-04 / FAMILY 331

L¹ 的 Daugavet 子空間中的精確漸近模數

逐篇導讀

對單位球在依測度收斂意義下全有界、且具有 Daugavet 性質的無窮維實 L¹ 子空間，本文算出平均中點與單側漸近模數，分別為 max{t/2,t−1}、max{0,t−2}。同一弱鄰域幾何排除全部等價 AUC 範數，並提供符合假設的定量構造。

問題

精確中點凸性與不可 AUC 重賦範如何共存。

條件與限制

需同時具有測度全有界性與 Daugavet 性質，不是所有 L¹ 子空間。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：作為比較不同漸近凸性指標的精確測試例。

Original English abstract

For an infinite-dimensional real subspace of L1 whose unit ball is totally bounded in measure and whose norm has the Daugavet property, we compute the averaged midpoint and one-sided asymptotic moduli at every unit center. They are $\max\{t/2,t-1\}$ and $\max\{0,t-2\}$, respectively. The same weak-neighborhood geometry excludes every equivalent asymptotically uniformly convex norm. A quantitative realization of the Kadets–Werner construction supplies a space with both hypotheses.

331-05 / FAMILY 331

由有界樹位勢與路徑成本導出的中點凸性

逐篇導讀

本文四種實 Banach 樹位勢空間，在 0<t<1 時滿足平均中點模數至少 √(1+t²/4)−1，卻均無 AUC 重賦範。有限高度樹的全域約束範數等於 Hilbert 向量與根路徑的最小加法成本；其中兩種外 Hilbert 和構造反身。

問題

樹位勢能否分離中點凸性與 AUC。

條件與限制

反身性只明確賦予所述二次路徑與線段起點外 Hilbert 和構造。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：把抽象凸性估計轉為可計算的路徑最佳化問題。

Original English abstract

Four real Banach spaces defined by bounded tree potentials satisfy the averaged asymptotic midpoint bound $\widehat\delta(t)\ge\sqrt{1+t^2/4}-1$ for $0\lt t\lt 1$, while none admits an asymptotically uniformly convex (AUC) renorming. On finite-height trees, the globally constrained norm equals the least additive cost of a Hilbert vector and root paths. The quadratic path and segment-start outer Hilbert sums are reflexive and asymptotically midpoint uniformly convex, and admit no equivalent AUC norm.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

331-07 / FAMILY 331

Original English abstract

332-01 / FAMILY 332

ℓ¹ 的度量 Markov 餘型二

本文主張實 ℓ¹ 具有度量 Markov 餘型二，回答 Mendel–Naor 問題。因而任意實 Hilbert 空間任意子集到 ℓ¹ 的 Lipschitz 映射，皆可延伸到全空間，Lipschitz 常數只損失普適乘數。

Original English abstract

We prove that the real Banach space ℓ1 has metric Markov cotype two, answering a question of Mendel and Naor. As a consequence, every Lipschitz map from an arbitrary subset of a real Hilbert space into ℓ1 extends to the whole Hilbert space with a universal multiplicative loss in its Lipschitz constant, resolving Ball's extension problem for this target.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

335-01 / FAMILY 335

實單純體積的積分純量曲率界

逐篇導讀

對每個 n≥3，本文主張存在只依 n 的 aₙ>0，使任意閉連通定向光滑 n 流形及光滑度量滿足 ∫(Scal⁻)^(n/2)dV≥aₙ‖M‖。Scal⁻=max{0,−Scal}，證明使用伴隨論文的非負純量曲率消失定理。

問題

Gromov 以負純量曲率積分控制實單純體積的猜想。

條件與限制

維度至少三；右側是實單純體積，常數不宣稱尖銳。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：由拓撲複雜度給出負曲率積分不可低於的門檻。

Original English abstract

We prove the integral scalar-curvature inequality proposed by Gromov. For every dimension n ≥ 3, there is a constant $a_n\gt 0$, depending only on n, such that $\displaystyle \int_M(\mathop{\mathrm{Scal}}\nolimits _g^-)^{n/2}\,dV_g\ge a_n\|M\|$ for every closed connected oriented smooth n-manifold M and every smooth Riemannian metric g. Here $\mathop{\mathrm{Scal}}\nolimits _g^-:=\max\{0,-\mathop{\mathrm{Scal}}\nolimits _g\}$, and $\|M\|$ is real simplicial volume. The proof uses the nonnegative-scalar-curvature vanishing theorem of the companion paper on rational inessentiality.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

340-01 / FAMILY 340

鄰近 Lagrangian 猜想的反例

逐篇導讀

對某個充分大的偶數 N，本文在 T*(S⁹×S^(N−1)) 構造閉、正合、光滑嵌入的 Lagrangian。它與底流形微分同胚，卻不與零截面 Hamiltonian 同痕。

無限制版本的鄰近 Lagrangian 猜想。

條件與限制

反例限指定高維餘切叢；微分同胚不保證 Hamiltonian 同痕。

可能延伸（推論）

潛在用途：分辨 Lagrangian 的光滑分類與辛同痕分類。

Original English abstract

We disprove the unrestricted nearby Lagrangian conjecture. For some sufficiently large even integer N, we construct a closed exact smoothly embedded Lagrangian in $T^*(S^9\times S^{N-1})$ that is diffeomorphic to the base but is not Hamiltonian isotopic to the zero section.

開啟本篇定理、章節路線與實作頁 · Original PDF · Source / BibTeX

341-01 / FAMILY 341

將 hypersymplectic 四維流形變形成超 Kähler 三重組

逐篇導讀

本文主張，閉連通定向四維流形上，每個光滑正規化正的閉二形式三重組，皆可光滑變形為超 Kähler 三重組。變形逐一保持三個上同調類。

Donaldson 的 hypersymplectic 變形猜想。

Original English abstract

Every smooth normalized positive triple of closed two-forms on a closed connected oriented four-manifold admits a smooth deformation, with each cohomology class fixed, to a hyperkähler triple. This resolves Donaldson's hypersymplectic deformation conjecture.

342-01 / FAMILY 342

本文主張，閉四維流形上的光滑概複結構，若被某辛形式馴服，就存在與它相容的辛形式。另以馴服錐不變部分閉包上的嚴格交數正性刻畫相容錐。

Donaldson 的 tamed-to-compatible 猜想。

條件與限制

Original English abstract

We prove that every smooth almost complex structure on a closed four-manifold which is tamed by a symplectic form is compatible with a symplectic form. This gives a positive solution to Donaldson's tamed-to-compatible conjecture.
